{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01H7iguPj6VkG15eVMZKRvR1","type":"tool_result","content":"20\tfrom typing import Any\n21\t\n22\tfrom .agent_invocations import create_agent_invocation_request\n23\tfrom .ledger import load_declared_jsonl\n24\tfrom .tool_registry import ensure_tools_dir\n25\t\n26\t\n27\t# Two distinct judge roles → two distinct judge_ids on the same finding. The\n28\t# kernel's consensus gate needs exactly this to ever fire on a tool finding.\n29\tJUDGE_FANOUT: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (\n30\t    (\"evidence_judgment\", \"aria-evidence-judge\"),\n31\t    (\"adversarial_judgment\", \"aria-adversarial-judge\"),\n32\t)\n33\t\n34\t# E14 — the arbiter that the `consensus_arbitration` role always named and\n35\t# nothing ever minted. The fan-out above guarantees two judges; when those two\n36\t# disagree the mechanical gate (`feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus`) records\n37\t# a `judge_disagreement` uncertainty and the finding's verdict stops there. The\n38\t# arbiter is the tie-break: it reads the two verdicts, applies the same\n39\t# consensus gate the kernel applies, and its answer lands in the SAME judgment\n40\t# group as a third calibrated vote, so a weighted gate can settle the finding.\n41\tCONSENSUS_ARBITRATION_ROLE: str = \"consensus_arbitration\"\n42\tCONSENSUS_ARBITER_AGENT: str = \"aria-consensus-arbiter\"\n43\t\n44\t# JJ-1 (ORPHAN-HIGH-731) - the SECOND reason to mint the arbiter: not a\n45\t# split, an ANCHOR. The split arm above only ever fires when the two judges\n46\t# disagree, so a consensus that will become ground truth was, until now,\n47\t# exactly the case nobody examined - agreement was treated as proof instead\n48\t# of as the thing to test. The anchor mint asks the arbiter to REFUTE a\n49\t# unanimous pair; surviving that is what promotes the row to ground truth\n50\t# (feedback_store.is_ground_truth_row). Same role, same agent, same\n51\t# (judgment_group_id, target_agent) idempotency key as the split arm, so a\n52\t# group can never carry two arbiters for two reasons.\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef _finding_key(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n56\t    \"\"\"The run-independent identity of the finding being judged.\"\"\"\n57\t    return str(item.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or item.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef _group_id(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n61\t    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — the judgment group is keyed by the FINDING, not the run.\n62\t\n63\t    The shipped key folded ``run_id`` in, so every nightly adapter run\n64\t    re-minted fresh envelopes for findings already sitting in the queue —\n65\t    462 minted, 42 accepted, 296 dead of anchor staleness in one week. With\n66\t    the run out of the key, the (group, agent) dedupe below becomes\n67\t    cross-night: a finding is asked once and re-examined only by the E9\n68\t    decision-questioning lane, which exists for exactly that.\n69\t    \"\"\"\n70\t    return f\"judge:{item.get('tool_id')}:{_finding_key(item)}\"\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef _render_prompt(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n74\t    return (\n75\t        \"Judge whether this adapter finding is a true_positive or false_positive.\\n\"\n76\t        f\"finding_id: {item.get('finding_id')}\\n\"\n77\t        f\"rule: {item.get('rule')}\\n\"\n78\t        f\"severity: {item.get('severity')}\\n\"\n79\t        f\"path: {item.get('path')}\\n\"\n80\t        f\"message: {item.get('message')}\\n\"\n81\t        \"Return verdict true_positive|false_positive with file:line evidence.\"\n82\t    )\n83\t\n84\t\n85\tdef _evidence_refs(item: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n86\t    refs: list[str] = []\n87\t    path = str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\")\n88\t    if path:\n89\t        refs.append(path)\n90\t    for ev in item.get(\"evidence\") or []:\n91\t        if isinstance(ev, str) and ev.strip():\n92\t            refs.append(ev)\n93\t    return refs\n94\t\n95\t\n96\tdef _existing_judge_dispatches(root: Path) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n97\t    \"\"\"Already-minted (judgment_group_id, target_agent) pairs. Keyed per-agent\n98\t    (not per-group) so a group left with only one judge — e.g. the second mint\n99\t    raised mid-loop last cycle — gets its missing judge minted next run instead\n100\t    of being skipped forever and starving consensus.\"\"\"\n101\t    try:\n102\t        rows = load_declared_jsonl(\n103\t            root / \"agent-invocations\" / \"requests.jsonl\",\n104\t            expected_surface=\"agent_invocation_requests\",\n105\t        )\n106\t    except Exception:\n107\t        return set()\n108\t    return {\n109\t        (str(r.get(\"judgment_group_id\")), str(r.get(\"target_agent\")))\n110\t        for r in rows\n111\t        if r.get(\"judgment_group_id\") and r.get(\"target_agent\")\n112\t    }\n113\t\n114\t\n115\tdef _judged_pairs(root: Path, tool_id: str) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n116\t    \"\"\"(finding_key, judge_id) pairs that already carry an ai_judge verdict.\n117\t\n118\t    A finding a judge has ALREADY answered must not be re-minted by the\n119\t    nightly fan-out regardless of ledger key drift — re-sampling closed\n120\t    verdicts is the decision-questioning lane's deliberate act, not the\n121\t    sampler's accident. Fingerprint preferred, finding_id fallback for\n122\t    rows recorded before fingerprints were threaded.\n123\t    \"\"\"\n124\t    from .feedback_store import load_feedback\n125\t\n126\t    pairs: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()\n127\t    try:\n128\t        rows = load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=root)\n129\t    except Exception:\n130\t        return pairs\n131\t    for row in rows:\n132\t        if row.get(\"source_type\") != \"ai_judge\":\n133\t            continue\n134\t        key = str(row.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or row.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n135\t        judge = str(row.get(\"judge_id\") or \"\")\n136\t        if key and judge:\n137\t            pairs.add((key, judge))\n138\t    return pairs\n139\t\n140\t\n141\tdef pending_judge_counts(*, base_dir: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n142\t    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — live (non-terminal) envelope count per judge role.\n143\t\n144\t    Bounded to the anchor window: anything older is ANCHOR_STALE by\n145\t    definition, so deriving its state would only re-prove it dead.\n146\t    \"\"\"\n147\t    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n148\t\n149\t    from .agent_invocations import derive_request_states, list_agent_invocation_requests\n150\t    from .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\n151\t\n152\t    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n153\t    horizon = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=4)\n154\t    counts: dict[str, int] = {role: 0 for role, _ in JUDGE_FANOUT}\n155\t    # ORPHAN-HIGH-794 — one batch derivation instead of a per-row derive\n156\t    # (each of which reloaded all three ledgers — the OOM churn class).\n157\t    states = derive_request_states(base_dir=root)\n158\t    for row in list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=root):\n159\t        role = str(row.get(\"role\") or \"\")"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f71484da-9cca-4504-a5c2-f8e1c65217d4","uuid":"76bddb32-e274-4489-b6a6-7935506df1b4","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:35:14.674Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-e340d37a0d97/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judge_fanout.py","content":"from typing import Any\n\nfrom .agent_invocations import create_agent_invocation_request\nfrom .ledger import load_declared_jsonl\nfrom .tool_registry import ensure_tools_dir\n\n\n# Two distinct judge roles → two distinct judge_ids on the same finding. The\n# kernel's consensus gate needs exactly this to ever fire on a tool finding.\nJUDGE_FANOUT: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (\n    (\"evidence_judgment\", \"aria-evidence-judge\"),\n    (\"adversarial_judgment\", \"aria-adversarial-judge\"),\n)\n\n# E14 — the arbiter that the `consensus_arbitration` role always named and\n# nothing ever minted. The fan-out above guarantees two judges; when those two\n# disagree the mechanical gate (`feedback_store.generate_ai_consensus`) records\n# a `judge_disagreement` uncertainty and the finding's verdict stops there. The\n# arbiter is the tie-break: it reads the two verdicts, applies the same\n# consensus gate the kernel applies, and its answer lands in the SAME judgment\n# group as a third calibrated vote, so a weighted gate can settle the finding.\nCONSENSUS_ARBITRATION_ROLE: str = \"consensus_arbitration\"\nCONSENSUS_ARBITER_AGENT: str = \"aria-consensus-arbiter\"\n\n# JJ-1 (ORPHAN-HIGH-731) - the SECOND reason to mint the arbiter: not a\n# split, an ANCHOR. The split arm above only ever fires when the two judges\n# disagree, so a consensus that will become ground truth was, until now,\n# exactly the case nobody examined - agreement was treated as proof instead\n# of as the thing to test. The anchor mint asks the arbiter to REFUTE a\n# unanimous pair; surviving that is what promotes the row to ground truth\n# (feedback_store.is_ground_truth_row). Same role, same agent, same\n# (judgment_group_id, target_agent) idempotency key as the split arm, so a\n# group can never carry two arbiters for two reasons.\n\n\ndef _finding_key(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"The run-independent identity of the finding being judged.\"\"\"\n    return str(item.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or item.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n\n\ndef _group_id(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — the judgment group is keyed by the FINDING, not the run.\n\n    The shipped key folded ``run_id`` in, so every nightly adapter run\n    re-minted fresh envelopes for findings already sitting in the queue —\n    462 minted, 42 accepted, 296 dead of anchor staleness in one week. With\n    the run out of the key, the (group, agent) dedupe below becomes\n    cross-night: a finding is asked once and re-examined only by the E9\n    decision-questioning lane, which exists for exactly that.\n    \"\"\"\n    return f\"judge:{item.get('tool_id')}:{_finding_key(item)}\"\n\n\ndef _render_prompt(item: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    return (\n        \"Judge whether this adapter finding is a true_positive or false_positive.\\n\"\n        f\"finding_id: {item.get('finding_id')}\\n\"\n        f\"rule: {item.get('rule')}\\n\"\n        f\"severity: {item.get('severity')}\\n\"\n        f\"path: {item.get('path')}\\n\"\n        f\"message: {item.get('message')}\\n\"\n        \"Return verdict true_positive|false_positive with file:line evidence.\"\n    )\n\n\ndef _evidence_refs(item: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n    refs: list[str] = []\n    path = str(item.get(\"path\") or \"\")\n    if path:\n        refs.append(path)\n    for ev in item.get(\"evidence\") or []:\n        if isinstance(ev, str) and ev.strip():\n            refs.append(ev)\n    return refs\n\n\ndef _existing_judge_dispatches(root: Path) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Already-minted (judgment_group_id, target_agent) pairs. Keyed per-agent\n    (not per-group) so a group left with only one judge — e.g. the second mint\n    raised mid-loop last cycle — gets its missing judge minted next run instead\n    of being skipped forever and starving consensus.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        rows = load_declared_jsonl(\n            root / \"agent-invocations\" / \"requests.jsonl\",\n            expected_surface=\"agent_invocation_requests\",\n        )\n    except Exception:\n        return set()\n    return {\n        (str(r.get(\"judgment_group_id\")), str(r.get(\"target_agent\")))\n        for r in rows\n        if r.get(\"judgment_group_id\") and r.get(\"target_agent\")\n    }\n\n\ndef _judged_pairs(root: Path, tool_id: str) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"(finding_key, judge_id) pairs that already carry an ai_judge verdict.\n\n    A finding a judge has ALREADY answered must not be re-minted by the\n    nightly fan-out regardless of ledger key drift — re-sampling closed\n    verdicts is the decision-questioning lane's deliberate act, not the\n    sampler's accident. Fingerprint preferred, finding_id fallback for\n    rows recorded before fingerprints were threaded.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .feedback_store import load_feedback\n\n    pairs: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()\n    try:\n        rows = load_feedback(tool_id=tool_id, base_dir=root)\n    except Exception:\n        return pairs\n    for row in rows:\n        if row.get(\"source_type\") != \"ai_judge\":\n            continue\n        key = str(row.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or row.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\")\n        judge = str(row.get(\"judge_id\") or \"\")\n        if key and judge:\n            pairs.add((key, judge))\n    return pairs\n\n\ndef pending_judge_counts(*, base_dir: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Y2 (ORPHAN-704) — live (non-terminal) envelope count per judge role.\n\n    Bounded to the anchor window: anything older is ANCHOR_STALE by\n    definition, so deriving its state would only re-prove it dead.\n    \"\"\"\n    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\n    from .agent_invocations import derive_request_states, list_agent_invocation_requests\n    from .agent_surface import TERMINAL_REQUEST_STATES\n\n    root = ensure_tools_dir(base_dir)\n    horizon = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=4)\n    counts: dict[str, int] = {role: 0 for role, _ in JUDGE_FANOUT}\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-794 — one batch derivation instead of a per-row derive\n    # (each of which reloaded all three ledgers — the OOM churn class).\n    states = derive_request_states(base_dir=root)\n    for row in list_agent_invocation_requests(base_dir=root):\n        role = str(row.get(\"role\") or \"\")","numLines":140,"startLine":20,"totalLines":661}}}

On live\n292\t        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n293\t        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n294\t        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n295\t        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n296\t        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n297\t        base_dir=base_dir,\n298\t    )\n299\t    return row\n300\t\n301\t\n302\tdef persist_supporting_payload("}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f71484da-9cca-4504-a5c2-f8e1c65217d4","uuid":"63f19f12-6fab-49a6-a66b-76c623f11126","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:35:37.599Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-e340d37a0d97/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judgment_bridge.py","content":"            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n        )\n\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n\n            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or arb_block.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n                reason=str(arb_reason),\n                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n        )\n    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n\n    note = (\n        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n    )\n    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n\n    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n\n    row = record_operator_feedback(\n        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n        run_id=str(run_id),\n        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n        verdict=verdict,\n        severity=severity,\n        note=note,\n        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n        # predate the stamp.\n        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n        rationale=str(note),\n        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n        base_dir=base_dir,\n    )\n    return row\n\n\ndef persist_supporting_payload(","numLines":125,"startLine":178,"totalLines":388}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HErLpuXqs9oA69paY2ew9Q","type":"tool_result","content":"2228\t    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n2229\t    #\n2230\t    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n2231\t    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n2232\t    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n2233\t    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n2234\t    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n2235\t    # which fails:\n2236\t    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n2237\t    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n2238\t    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n2239\t    #\n2240\t    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n2241\t    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n2242\t    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n2243\t    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n2244\t    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n2245\t    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n2246\t    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n2247\t    #\n2248\t    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n2249\t    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n2250\t    # internal kernel identity fields.\n2251\t    if completed.stdout:\n2252\t        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2253\t            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n2254\t            request_id=request_id,\n2255\t            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n2256\t            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n2257\t            role=role,\n2258\t            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n2259\t            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n2260\t            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n2261\t        )\n2262\t        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n2263\t        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2264\t        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n2265\t        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n2266\t        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n2267\t        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n2268\t        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n2269\t        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n2270\t        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n2271\t        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n2272\t        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f71484da-9cca-4504-a5c2-f8e1c65217d4","uuid":"bca04085-c2f5-4308-a2e9-44f8467a5757","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:36:11.307Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-e340d37a0d97/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"    # Plan ARIA-V7 §2g v2 + V7.10 envelope-extraction fix.\n    #\n    # WHY: claude -p stream-json emits JSONL events\n    # ({\"result\": \"...\", \"total_cost_usd\": ..., ...}) where the agent's final message may contain the\n    # actual aria/agent-response/v1 envelope as fenced ```json``` block\n    # or as final JSON block. Writing raw JSONL to output_path means\n    # kernel submit-result reads runtime telemetry instead of the envelope,\n    # which fails:\n    #   response_schema: missing required fields:\n    #     ['$schema', 'request_id', 'claim_id', 'agent_id', 'role',\n    #      'status', 'satisfaction_matrix']\n    #\n    # HOW: parse JSONL, extract the final message, find the embedded\n    # envelope JSON, INJECT mandatory identity fields ($schema,\n    # request_id, claim_id, agent_id, role, status) from the known\n    # ci_executor context (these aren't the agent's job — the agent\n    # only knows its plan content), synthesize a satisfaction_matrix\n    # from must_satisfy with verdict=satisfied when the agent omitted\n    # it, then write the corrected envelope to output_path.\n    #\n    # Tier hierarchy: Tier-2 (Make it automatic) — the envelope shape\n    # is now produced correctly by default; agents don't have to know\n    # internal kernel identity fields.\n    if completed.stdout:\n        envelope = _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n            raw_stdout=completed.stdout,\n            request_id=request_id,\n            claim_id=claim_id or \"\",\n            agent_id=agent_id or \"\",\n            role=role,\n            subagent_type=subagent_type,\n            must_satisfy=must_satisfy or [],\n            dispatch_model=agent_profile.model,\n        )\n        envelope[\"details\"][\"agent_contract_hash\"] = agent_contract.contract_hash\n        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        _write_sanitized_envelope(output_path, envelope)\n        resolved_transcript_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        resolved_transcript_path.write_text(completed.stdout, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        # Plan ARIA-V3.1-D3 — per-LLM-call cost attribution. Gated on\n        # _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY frozen sentinel (V3.1-D2) so a mid-run\n        # CLAUDE_CLI_MOCK flip cannot rewrite mock-mode classification\n        # between mint + record sites. request_envelope + tools_dir\n        # MUST both be supplied for the record to fire — None defaults\n        # preserve V8 backward-compat for callers that haven't migrated.","numLines":45,"startLine":2228,"totalLines":5265}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VLzvJVPTncdpix1UkvuyeB","type":"tool_result","content":"2496:def _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2497-    *,\n2498-    raw_stdout: str,\n2499-    request_id: str,\n2500-    claim_id: str,\n2501-    agent_id: str,\n2502-    role: str,\n2503-    subagent_type: str,\n2504-    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2505-    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n2506-) -> dict[str, Any]:\n2507-    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n2508-\n2509-    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n2510-    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n2511-    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n2512-    \"\"\"\n2513-    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n2514-    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n2515-    if not agent_text:\n2516-        agent_text = raw_stdout\n2517-    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n2518-\n2519-    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n2520-        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n2521-        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2522-        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n2523-        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n2524-        \"role\": role,\n2525-        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n2526-    }\n2527-\n2528-    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n2529-    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n2530-        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n2531-    else:\n2532-        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n2533-        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). Claim (2) is structurally verified against the worktree at b8febe123: apps/messaging-service/src/health/ holds exactly two files (health.controller.ts and health.module.ts); the only importer of ./health.controller anywhere in the repository is apps/messaging-service/src/health/health.module.ts:9; no *.spec.ts under apps/messaging-service names HealthController; and the seven same-named HealthController specs in sibling services each import their own relative ../health.controller (e.g. apps/farm-service/src/health/__tests__/health.controller.spec.ts:12), so they cannot compile or exercise messaging's class. Under the adapter's own matcher (tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:296-320: same basename, same directory, sibling __tests__, or a resolved import) the match set is empty, which is exactly the condition that emits this rule at lines 187-201. CLAIM (1): WHY THE FILE IS GENUINELY SECURITY-SENSITIVE, NOT A REGEX ACCIDENT. The classifier at test-gap-adapter.ts:271-272 fired on file text, not path: the only matches are the tokens @Public() and TenantGuard inside the JSDoc comment at health.controller.ts:6; the file writes no guard or auth decorator itself. That trigger is fragile, but the conclusion is correct on behaviour: line 25 declares @Controller('health') and line 26 extends StandardHealthController, which is decorated @Controller('health') @Public() @SkipThrottle() at libs/backend-common/src/health/standard-health.controller.ts:111-113. Public() sets the isPublic and skipTenantGuard metadata keys (libs/backend-common/src/decorators/roles.decorator.ts:149-157); NestJS reads class-level metadata through the prototype chain, so this subclass is the concrete registration of an unauthenticated, tenant-guard-bypassing, unthrottled HTTP surface inside messaging-service. On that surface it adds behaviour of its own: the Redis ping() and NATS connect() probes at lines 53-83, with four branches (provider absent -> key omitted; provider present and failing -> 'error') that no test in the repository executes. The parent's spec (libs/backend-common/src/health/__tests__/standard-health.controller.spec.ts:171-242) covers the generic getAdditionalChecks() merge with a synthetic subclass, not this one, and its decorator test at line 246 asserts only the route path, never IS_PUBLIC_KEY; the farm-service spec (lines 150-155) does the same. WHY IT MATTERS AND WHAT BREAKS IF SKIPPED. A public probe endpoint is one of the few surfaces reachable without a token, and it is also what the container orchestrator uses to decide whether to kill the pod or route traffic to it. A regression in this file — an error escaping the try/catch, a wrong aggregation, or a class-level decorator that re-arms a guard or throttle — would either take messaging-service out of rotation or change what an anonymous caller can learn about Redis/NATS topology, and CI would stay green because nothing imports this class. Downstream surface: messaging-service liveness/readiness plus the anonymous information-disclosure boundary. CALIBRATION. Confidence is 0.84 rather than higher because the classifier matched a comment rather than code (the same gap in a sibling lacking that comment would go undetected — a precision defect in the adapter, not in this finding); the rule's matcher accepts any adjacent test, so the name 'security test' overstates what it measures; and dedicated health specs exist for seven of the fifteen apps/*/src/health/health.controller.ts files, so messaging is one of eight sharing this gap rather than a lone regression. None of these makes the finding false. CORRECT PATH (architectural tier 3, make it detectable). Add apps/messaging-service/src/health/__tests__/health.controller.spec.ts in the shape of apps/farm-service/src/health/__tests__/health.controller.spec.ts, providing mocks for DataSource, REDIS_CLIENT and NATS_SERVICE, and asserting: 200/'ok' when every check passes; 200/'degraded' with redis:'error' when ping() rejects; 200/'degraded' with nats:'error' when connect() rejects; the redis and nats keys absent when the optional providers are not injected; 503/'not_ready' when every check fails; Reflect.getMetadata('path', HealthController) === 'health'; and Reflect.getMetadata(IS_PUBLIC_KEY, HealthController) === true so the inherited public posture becomes an explicit, pinned security assertion.
